GERVASII TILBERIENSIS OTIA IMPERIALIA (Recreation for an Emperor)
Gervase of Tilbury (c. 1150-1228), written c. 1211 for Emperor Otto IV
========================================================================
PROVENANCE NOTE (Tower of Babel staging): Complete Latin text after G. W. Leibniz,
"Scriptores rerum Brunsvicensium", vol. 1 (Hanover, 1707), pp. 884-1004, and the
"Emendationes et supplementa" in vol. 2 (1710), pp. 751-784. Public domain scans
(Google/Internet Archive). The work is complete: the three decisiones (I: the
Creation and ancient history; II: the kingdoms of the world; III: the book of
marvels), the closing letter "Exitus operis", Leibniz's emendanda, and the vol. 2
supplements from additional manuscripts. Period typography preserved (long s
rendered as f in the OCR).
========================================================================

[FROM LEIBNIZ VOL. 1]
